<script>(()=>{
const raf=window.requestAnimationFrame.bind(window);
const F=window.__uk={on:false,cap:40,min:30,res:1,fps:60,raf,pt:-1,ok:1,nx:0,fr:0,t0:performance.now(),hi:0,R:null,base:1,cur:0,ctxs:[]};
F.apply=()=>{if(!F.R)return;const r=F.base*F.res;if(Math.abs(r-F.cur)>.02){F.cur=r;try{F.R.setPixelRatio(r)}catch(e){}}};
// adaptive resolution: if the game drops under the 30fps floor, shrink render resolution until it recovers
function adapt(){
 if(!F.on||!F.R)return;
 const lo=F.fps<F.min+1.5&&F.cap>=F.min+4,hi=F.fps>F.cap-3;
 if(lo){F.res=Math.max(.3,F.res*.87);F.hi=0}
 else if(hi&&F.res<1){if(++F.hi>=4){F.res=Math.min(1,F.res+.05);F.hi=0}}
 else F.hi=0;
 F.apply();
}
// hard cap (default 40fps): all callbacks in the same vsync share one pass/skip decision
window.requestAnimationFrame=cb=>raf(function f(t){
 if(t!==F.pt){
  F.pt=t;
  if(!F.on)F.ok=1;
  else{
   if(!F.nx||t-F.nx>120)F.nx=t;
   F.ok=t>=F.nx-4;
   if(F.ok)F.nx=Math.max(F.nx+1000/F.cap,t+1);
  }
  if(F.ok)F.fr++;
  const dt=t-F.t0;
  if(dt>=600){if(dt<3000){F.fps=F.fr*1000/dt;adapt()}F.fr=0;F.t0=t}
 }
 if(!F.ok){raf(f);return}
 cb(t);
});
// grab the game's renderer so resolution can be scaled
try{
 const P=THREE.WebGLRenderer.prototype,o=P.render;
 P.render=function(a,b){
  if(!F.R&&this.domElement&&this.domElement.id!=='ukSC'){F.R=this;F.base=this.getPixelRatio();F.cur=F.base}
  return o.call(this,a,b);
 };
}catch(e){}
// tap game audio so "Record game" can capture sound
try{
 const oc=AudioNode.prototype.connect;
 AudioNode.prototype.connect=function(d,...a){
  try{
   if(d instanceof AudioDestinationNode&&!d.context.__skip){
    const c=d.context;
    if(!c.__ukD){c.__ukD=c.createMediaStreamDestination();F.ctxs.push(c)}
    oc.call(this,c.__ukD,...a);
   }
  }catch(e){}
  return oc.call(this,d,...a);
 };
}catch(e){}
})();
</script>

function ember(p,c,n,sp=9,sz=.9){for(let i=0;i<n&&pt.length<700;i++){const m=new T.Mesh(pg,mat(c));m.position.set(p.x+(rnd()-.5)*.4,p.y+(rnd()-.5)*.4,p.z+(rnd()-.5)*.4);m.scale.setScalar(sz*(.6+rnd()));wd.add(m);pt.push({m,vx:(rnd()-.5)*sp,vy:rnd()*sp,vz:(rnd()-.5)*sp,t:.5+rnd()*.6})}}

function smokeP(p,n=6){for(let i=0;i<n&&smk.length<130;i++){const s=1.5+rnd()*2,m=new T.Sprite(new T.SpriteMaterial({map:GT,color:0x333338,transparent:true,opacity:.5,depthWrite:false}));m.scale.set(s,s,1);m.position.set(p.x+(rnd()-.5),p.y+rnd()*.5,p.z+(rnd()-.5));m.userData.nogib=1;wd.add(m);smk.push({m,vx:(rnd()-.5)*2,vy:1.5+rnd()*2,vz:(rnd()-.5)*2,t:1+rnd()*.8,ttl:1.8,s})}}

/* blood v6: droplets stretch with velocity, collide with floor/walls/props, splat into decals */
const bzG=new T.SphereGeometry(.1,6,4),bzC=[0xc01212,0x8e0b0b,0x5c0707],bzV=new T.Vector3(),bzP=new T.Vector3(),bzD=new T.Vector3(),bzZ=new T.Vector3(0,0,1);let bzQ0,bzQ1,bzQn,bzQs,bzDn=0,BZCAP=560,ROOF=0;
function bzSl(a,d,lo,hi,i){if(Math.abs(d)<1e-9)return a>=lo&&a<=hi;let ta=(lo-a)/d,tb=(hi-a)/d,s=-1;if(ta>tb){const z=ta;ta=tb;tb=z;s=1}if(ta>bzQ0){bzQ0=ta;bzQn=i;bzQs=s}if(tb<bzQ1)bzQ1=tb;return bzQ0<=bzQ1}
function bzSeg(a,d,b){bzQ0=0;bzQ1=1;bzQn=-1;return bzSl(a.x,d.x,b.min.x,b.max.x,0)&&bzSl(a.y,d.y,b.min.y,b.max.y,1)&&bzSl(a.z,d.z,b.min.z,b.max.z,2)&&bzQn>=0}
function bzDrop(x,y,z,vx,vy,vz,s,dr){if(pt.length>=BZCAP)return;const m=new T.Mesh(bzG,mat(bzC[rnd()*3|0]));m.position.set(x,y,z);m.scale.setScalar(s);wd.add(m);pt.push({m,bz:1,vx,vy,vz,s,dr,t:1.5+rnd()*.9})}
function bloodFX(p,n,dx,dz){if(dx==null){dx=p.x-P.x;dz=p.z-P.z}const l=Math.hypot(dx,dz)||1;dx/=l;dz/=l;const gore=n>=14,c=Math.round(n*(M.perf?1.4:2.4)*GFX.gore);
 for(let i=0;i<c;i++){const mist=rnd()<.45,a=rnd()*6.283,sp=mist?10+rnd()*10:3+rnd()*rnd()*(gore?15:10);let vx,vy,vz;
  if(gore&&rnd()<.5){vx=Math.cos(a)*sp;vz=Math.sin(a)*sp;vy=3+rnd()*sp*.9}else{vx=(dx+(rnd()-.5)*1.1)*sp;vz=(dz+(rnd()-.5)*1.1)*sp;vy=(.15+rnd()*.7)*sp}
  bzDrop(p.x+(rnd()-.5)*.3,p.y+(rnd()-.5)*.3,p.z+(rnd()-.5)*.3,vx,vy,vz,mist?.45+rnd()*.35:.8+rnd()*.9,mist?1.6:.3)}}
function bzStep(q,dt){const m=q.m,p=m.position;q.vy-=22*dt;const k=Math.exp(-q.dr*dt);q.vx*=k;q.vy*=k;q.vz*=k;bzP.copy(p);bzV.set(q.vx*dt,q.vy*dt,q.vz*dt);let bt=2,bn=-1;
 for(const o of sol){if(!o.parent||!o.visible)continue;const b=o.userData.bb||(o.userData.bb=new T.Box3().setFromObject(o));if(b.max.x<Math.min(bzP.x,bzP.x+bzV.x)-.1||b.min.x>Math.max(bzP.x,bzP.x+bzV.x)+.1||b.max.z<Math.min(bzP.z,bzP.z+bzV.z)-.1||b.min.z>Math.max(bzP.z,bzP.z+bzV.z)+.1)continue;if(bzSeg(bzP,bzV,b)&&bzQ0<bt){bt=bzQ0;bn=bzQn*2+(bzQs>0?1:0)}}
 if(bn>=0){const x=bzP.x+bzV.x*bt,y=bzP.y+bzV.y*bt,z=bzP.z+bzV.z*bt,ax=bn>>1,sg=bn&1?1:-1,nx=ax==0?sg:0,ny=ax==1?sg:0,nz=ax==2?sg:0,sp=Math.hypot(q.vx,q.vy,q.vz);
  if(ny>-.5&&bzDn<7&&rnd()<.3+q.s*.15){bzDn++;decal(new T.Vector3(x,y,z),nx,ny,nz,.3+q.s*.35+rnd()*.35,ny>.5?1:0)}
  if(sp>9&&q.s>.7&&rnd()<.4)for(let i=0;i<2;i++)bzDrop(x+nx*.05,y+ny*.05,z+nz*.05,nx*sp*.18+(rnd()-.5)*3,ny*sp*.18+1+rnd()*2,nz*sp*.18+(rnd()-.5)*3,q.s*.45,1.2);
  wd.remove(m);pt.splice(pt.indexOf(q),1);return}
 p.add(bzV);const sp=Math.hypot(q.vx,q.vy,q.vz);
 if(sp>2.5){bzD.set(q.vx/sp,q.vy/sp,q.vz/sp);m.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(bzZ,bzD);m.scale.set(q.s,q.s,q.s*Math.min(5,1+sp*.14))}
 if(p.y<-3||(q.t-=dt)<=0){wd.remove(m);pt.splice(pt.indexOf(q),1)}}
function bzGib(q,bleed){const p=q.m.position;
 if(bleed){if(pt.length>520||Math.hypot(q.vx,q.vy,q.vz)<3)return;bzDrop(p.x,p.y,p.z,q.vx*.2+(rnd()-.5)*3,q.vy*.1+(rnd()-.5)*2,q.vz*.2+(rnd()-.5)*3,.7+rnd()*.6,.5);return}

function explode(p,r,dmg){
 flashFX(p,r*.9,0xffb060);ringFX(p,r,0xffc060);
 ember(p,0xffa030,20,13,1.4);ember(p,0xff5020,12,9,1);smokeP(p,10);
 xl.position.copy(p);xl.intensity=5;shk=Math.max(shk,.9);snd(60,.4,'sawtooth',.4);
 if(dmg>0)for(const e of en.slice()){const d=Math.hypot(p.x-e.x,p.y-e.y-e.h/2,p.z-e.z);if(d<r+1)hit(e,dmg*(1-d/(r+3)),new T.Vector3(e.x,e.y+e.h/2,e.z))}
 for(const b of bar.slice())if(!b.q&&Math.hypot(p.x-b.x,p.z-b.z)<r&&p.y<r){b.q=1;setTimeout(()=>blowBar(b),110)}
 const l=Math.hypot(p.x-P.x,p.y-P.y-1,p.z-P.z);
 if(l<r+2){const q=(1-l/(r+3))*30,i=l||1;P.vx+=(P.x-p.x)/i*q;P.vz+=(P.z-p.z)/i*q;P.vy+=(P.y+1-p.y)/i*q+6;P.gr=0}}
// ---------- combat ----------
function spawn(t){const[h,v,w,hh,c]=E[t],s=S[st][3],a=rnd()*6.283,r=window.k7B?18+rnd()*10:s*.55+rnd()*s*.3;
 const{g,p}=MK[t](),e={t,hp:h*(1+.35*lv)*(1+st*.04),v,x:(window.k7B?P.x:0)+Math.cos(a)*r,y:t==2||t==4?4+rnd()*2:0,z:(window.k7B?P.z:0)+Math.sin(a)*r,m:g,parts:p,h:hh,w,cd:1+rnd()*2,sc:1,f:0,ph:rnd()*6,tm:0,atkT:0,mov:0,dead:0,pin:0,hitCount:0};
 g.rotation.order="YXZ";g.userData.e=e;g.traverse(o=>o.userData.e=e);
 e.flash=sprt(0xffffff,hh*1.7);e.flash.position.y=hh/2;e.flash.material.opacity=0;e.flash.userData.nogib=1;g.add(e.flash);
 const sh=new T.Mesh(shg,shm);sh.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;wd.add(sh);e.sh=sh;
 wd.add(g);en.push(e);burstFX(new T.Vector3(e.x,.4,e.z),6,0x8a8a9a,4,1)}
function bul(x,y,z,tx,ty,tz,sp,own,dmg,wi2=-1){const dx=tx-x,dy=ty-y,dz=tz-z,l=Math.hypot(dx,dy,dz)||1,m=new T.Mesh(spg,mat(own?0xffe060:0xff7a2a));m.position.set(x,y,z);m.scale.setScalar(own?1.4:1.1);const gl=sprt(own?0xffd070:0xff5030,1);gl.userData.nogib=1;m.add(gl);wd.add(m);pj.push({m,x,y,z,vx:dx/l*sp,vy:dy/l*sp,vz:dz/l*sp,own,dmg,l:8,wi:wi2,gravity:own?0:0.35})}
function rmp(p){pj.splice(pj.indexOf(p),1);wd.remove(p.m)}
function gib(e){
 const parts=[];e.m.traverse(o=>{if(o.isMesh&&!o.userData.nogib)parts.push(o)});
 const p=new T.Vector3(e.x,e.y+e.h/2,e.z);
 bloodFX(p,e.t>2?26:14);glowP(p,0xff4030,4,4,1.5);

function animE(e,dt){
 const p=e.parts;e.tm+=dt*(1+e.v*.12);
 const mv=e.mov,w=e.tm*(e.t==3?4.5:7);
 if(e.t==0){
  p.legL.rotation.x=Math.sin(w)*.75*mv;p.legR.rotation.x=-Math.sin(w)*.75*mv;
  let a=-1.15+Math.sin(e.tm*3)*.08*mv;
  if(e.atkT>0){const q=1-e.atkT/.32;a=-1.15-Math.sin(q*Math.PI)*1.2;p.head.rotation.x=-.15-Math.sin(q*Math.PI)*.4}
  else p.head.rotation.x=-.15+Math.sin(w*2+1)*.05;
  p.armL.rotation.x=a;p.armR.rotation.x=a+Math.sin(e.tm*3+1)*.08*mv;
  p.torso.rotation.x=.3+Math.sin(w*2)*.06*mv;
 }else if(e.t==1){
  p.legL.rotation.x=Math.sin(w)*.55*mv;p.legR.rotation.x=-Math.sin(w)*.55*mv;
  p.armL.rotation.x=-Math.sin(w)*.4*mv-.2;
  p.torso.rotation.x=.08+Math.sin(w*2)*.03;
  p.head.rotation.y=Math.sin(e.tm*.7)*.2;
  p.gunArm.rotation.x=e.atkT>0?-Math.sin((1-e.atkT/.3)*Math.PI)*.28:0;
 }else if(e.t==2){
  p.core.rotation.y+=dt*2.2;p.core.rotation.x=Math.sin(e.tm*1.5)*.15;
  p.ring.rotation.z+=dt*(e.atkT>0?16:1.5+mv);
  p.eye.material.emissiveIntensity=e.atkT>0?2.5+Math.sin(e.tm*40)*1.5:.9;
 }else if(e.t==3){
  p.legL.rotation.x=Math.sin(w)*.5*mv;p.legR.rotation.x=-Math.sin(w)*.5*mv;
  let ar=-.25+Math.sin(w)*.25*mv;
  if(e.atkT>0){const q=1-e.atkT/.42;ar=q<.5?-.25-q*4.5:-2.5+(q-.5)*6;
   if(q>=.5&&!e.slam){e.slam=1;ringFX(new T.Vector3(e.x,.15,e.z),3,0xff6040);snd(90,.2,'square',.2)}}
  else e.slam=0;
  p.armL.rotation.x=ar;p.armR.rotation.x=ar;
  p.torso.rotation.x=.12+Math.sin(w*2)*.05*mv;
 }else{
  p.body.position.y=3.1+Math.sin(e.tm*1.4)*.25;

 }}

/* wall between two points (2D, against the level boxes) - used to muffle far/occluded sounds */
function boxHit(o,x0,z0,x1,z1){let a=0,b=1;const dx=x1-x0,dz=z1-z0;for(let i=0;i<2;i++){const p=i?z0:x0,d=i?dz:dx,lo=i?o.z-o.d/2:o.x-o.w/2,hi=i?o.z+o.d/2:o.x+o.w/2;if(Math.abs(d)<1e-9){if(p<lo||p>hi)return false}else{let t1=(lo-p)/d,t2=(hi-p)/d;if(t1>t2){const q=t1;t1=t2;t2=q}a=Math.max(a,t1);b=Math.min(b,t2);if(a>b)return false}}return true}

function addFiles(files){
 [...files].forEach(f=>{
  const t=f.type.startsWith('image')?'image':f.type.startsWith('video')||/\.(mp4|webm|mov|mkv)$/i.test(f.name)?'video':'audio';
  addItem({name:f.name,type:t,url:URL.createObjectURL(f)});
  say('','Imported '+t+': '+f.name,'s');
 });
}
function pick(){const i=E('input',{type:'file',multiple:true,accept:'audio/*,video/*,image/*,.mp3,.mp4,.webm,.wav,.ogg,.png,.jpg,.gif,.webp'});i.onchange=()=>addFiles(i.files);i.click()}

// ---------- jumpscare + crash detection ----------
let lastScare=0;window.k7Flash=()=>flash();
function flash(){
 const o=E('div');o.style.cssText='position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:2147483647;background:#000 url('+A.call+') center/contain no-repeat;pointer-events:none';
 D.body.append(o);

}
function scare(why,force){
 const n=Date.now();if(!force&&n-lastScare<15000)return;lastScare=n;
 flash();say('','Crash attempt blocked ('+why+')','s');
}
// ;jumpscare [victim] - no victim = yourself. Victim = a connected multiplayer player (name, or ID, or name prefix)
function jumpscare(v){
 v=(v||'').trim();
 if(!v||/^(me|self|myself)$/i.test(v)||v.toLowerCase()===nm.toLowerCase()){flash();say('','Jumpscared yourself.','s');return}
 const p=MP();if(!p){say('','Multiplayer is not available.','s');return}
 const L=p.players(),q=v.toLowerCase();
 let m=L.filter(x=>x.name.toLowerCase()===q||x.id.toLowerCase()===q);
 if(!m.length)m=L.filter(x=>x.name.toLowerCase().startsWith(q));
 if(!m.length){say('','No player "'+v+'" online'+(L.length?' ('+L.map(x=>x.name).join(', ')+')':' - join someone in the MULTIPLAYER panel first')+'.','s');return}
 const sid=Math.random().toString(36).slice(2);
 m.forEach(x=>{p.send(x.id,{t:'scare',m:sid});setTimeout(()=>p.send(x.id,{t:'scare',m:sid}),150)});
 say('','Jumpscared '+m.map(x=>x.name).join(', ')+'.','s');
}
let errs=[];
const bad=/call stack|out of memory|invalid (array|string) length|allocation failed|too much recursion/i;
const onErr=m=>{
 const n=performance.now();errs=errs.filter(t=>n-t<3000);errs.push(n);
 if(bad.test(String(m))||errs.length>30)scare('error flood');
};
window.addEventListener('error',e=>onErr(e.message));
window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',e=>onErr(e.reason&&e.reason.message||e.reason));
window.addEventListener('webglcontextlost',()=>scare('GPU context lost'),true);
let tick=performance.now(),vis=!D.hidden;
D.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{vis=!D.hidden;tick=performance.now()});
setInterval(()=>{
 const n=performance.now();
 if(vis&&!D.hidden&&n>15000&&n-tick>5000)scare('freeze');
 tick=n;
 const m=performance.memory;if(m&&m.usedJSHeapSize>m.jsHeapSizeLimit*.92)scare('memory');
},1000);

// ---------- game recorder ----------
let gr=null,gc=0;
function recGame(want){
 if(gr){
  if(want===true){say('','Already recording.','s');return}
  try{gr.stop()}catch(e){gr=null;rb.textContent='Record game'}
  return;
 }
 if(want===false){say('','Not recording.','s');return}
 const cv=F.R?F.R.domElement:[...D.querySelectorAll('canvas')].filter(c=>c.id!=='ukSC'&&c.id!=='mf').sort((a,b)=>b.width*b.height-a.width*a.height)[0];
 if(!cv||!cv.captureStream){say('','No game canvas to record.','s');return}
 const st=new MediaStream(cv.captureStream(30).getVideoTracks());
 const cx=F.ctxs[0];if(cx&&cx.__ukD)cx.__ukD.stream.getAudioTracks().forEach(t=>st.addTrack(t));
 const ch=[],m=mime();
 gr=new MediaRecorder(st,m?{mimeType:m,videoBitsPerSecond:6e6}:{});
 gr.ondataavailable=e=>e.data.size&&ch.push(e.data);
 gr.onstop=()=>{
  const b=new Blob(ch,{type:'video/webm'}),n='Game clip '+(++gc);
  addItem({name:n,type:'video',url:URL.createObjectURL(b)});dl(b,n.replace(' ','_')+'.webm');
  say('','Saved "'+n+'" (downloaded + in your media library).','s');gr=null;rb.textContent='Record game';
 };
 gr.start(1000);rb.textContent='Stop recording';say('','Recording started. Type ;record again to stop and save.','s');
}

function human(c,e){

}

function add(v){
 init();

  const it=tracks_all()[+v.slice(1)];if(!it)return;o.kind=it.type;o.name=it.name;
  if(it.type==='image'){
   m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1,1),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({transparent:true,side:THREE.DoubleSide}));
   new THREE.TextureLoader().load(it.url,t=>{m.material.map=t;m.material.needsUpdate=true;o.base.set(2*t.image.width/t.image.height,2,1);m.scale.copy(o.base).multiplyScalar(o.s)});
   o.base.set(2,2,1);m.scale.copy(o.base);m.position.y=1.2;
  }else{
   const el=E('video',{src:it.url,playsInline:true,preload:'auto'});o.el=el;route(el);
   if(it.type==='video'){
    const t=new THREE.VideoTexture(el);o.tex=t;
    m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1,1),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({map:t,side:THREE.DoubleSide}));
    el.onloadedmetadata=()=>{o.base.set(2.4*el.videoWidth/el.videoHeight,2.4,1);m.scale.copy(o.base).multiplyScalar(o.s)};
    o.base.set(4.2,2.4,1);m.scale.copy(o.base);m.position.y=1.5;
   }
  }
 }
 o.mesh=m;if(m){scene.add(m)}
 S.O.push(o);S.sel=o;
 if(m&&HUM[v])m.traverse(q=>{if(q!==m&&q.name)S.O.push({id:++lid,name:o.name+' / '+q.name,kind:'part',mesh:q,keys:[],s:1,base:q.scale.clone(),owner:o})});
 list();props();
}
const tracks_all=()=>LIB;
function ev(o,t){
 const k=o.keys;if(!k.length||!o.mesh)return;
 let a=k[0],b=k[k.length-1];
 if(t<=a.t)b=a;else if(t>=b.t)a=b;else for(let i=0;i<k.length-1;i++)if(t>=k[i].t&&t<=k[i+1].t){a=k[i];b=k[i+1];break}
 const u=b.t===a.t?0:(t-a.t)/(b.t-a.t),L=(x,y)=>x+(y-x)*u,d=Math.PI/180;
 o.mesh.position.set(L(a.p[0],b.p[0]),L(a.p[1],b.p[1]),L(a.p[2],b.p[2]));
 o.mesh.rotation.set(L(a.r[0],b.r[0])*d,L(a.r[1],b.r[1])*d,L(a.r[2],b.r[2])*d);
 o.s=L(a.s,b.s);if(o.kind!=='cam')o.mesh.scale.copy(o.base).multiplyScalar(o.s);
}
function key(){
 const o=S.sel;if(!o||!o.mesh)return;const m=o.mesh,r=180/Math.PI,t=+S.T.toFixed(2);
 o.keys=o.keys.filter(k=>Math.abs(k.t-t)>.02);
 o.keys.push({t,p:[m.position.x,m.position.y,m.position.z],r:[m.rotation.x*r,m.rotation.y*r,m.rotation.z*r],s:o.s});
 o.keys.sort((a,b)=>a.t-b.t);marks();
}
function marks(){const k=$('#ukKs');k.innerHTML='';(S.sel&&S.sel.keys||[]).forEach(q=>k.append(E('i',{style:'left:'+q.t/S.dur*100+'%'})))}
function list(){
 const L=$('#sL');L.innerHTML='';
 S.O.forEach(o=>{const d=E('div',{className:'it'+(o===S.sel?' on':''),textContent:(o.kind==='part'?'   - ':'')+o.name+'  ['+o.kind+']',onclick:()=>{S.sel=o;list();props()}});
  d.append(E('button',{innerHTML:ic('play'),title:'Play from this object\'s first key',style:'float:right;padding:0 6px',onclick:e=>{e.stopPropagation();const k=(o.owner||o).keys;S.sel=o;S.T=k.length?k[0].t:(o.start||0);aT=-1;setPlay(true);list();props()}}));L.append(d)});
 marks();
}
function num(lab,get,set,step){
 const i=E('input',{type:'number',step:step||.1,value:+get().toFixed(3)});i.oninput=()=>{const v=parseFloat(i.value);if(!isNaN(v))set(v)};
 const l=E('label',{textContent:lab});l.append(i);return l;
}
function props(){
 const p=$('#sP'),o=S.sel;p.innerHTML='<h4>PROPERTIES</h4>';if(!o)return;
 const n=E('input',{value:o.name,oninput:()=>{o.name=n.value;list()}});p.append(E('label',{textContent:'Name'},n));
 if(o.mesh){
  const m=o.mesh,d=Math.PI/180;
  [['Position',m.position,1,.1],['Rotation (deg)',m.rotation,180/Math.PI,5]].forEach(([t,v,k,st])=>{
   p.append(E('label',{textContent:t}));const r=E('div',{className:'r3'});
   ['x','y','z'].forEach(a=>r.append(num(a,()=>v[a]*k,x=>{v[a]=x/k},st)));p.append(r);
  });
  if(o.kind!=='cam'){p.append(num('Scale',()=>o.s,x=>{o.s=x;m.scale.copy(o.base).multiplyScalar(x)},.1));p.append(E('label',{textContent:'Size XYZ'}));const r=E('div',{className:'r3'});['x','y','z'].forEach(a=>r.append(num(a,()=>o.base[a],x=>{o.base[a]=Math.max(.01,x);m.scale.copy(o.base).multiplyScalar(o.s)},.1)));p.append(r)}
 }
 if(o.el)p.append(num('Starts at (s)',()=>o.start,x=>{o.start=x},.1));
 p.append(E('p',{style:'color:#889;font-size:12px',textContent:'Fly: WASD (Q/E down/up, Shift fast), right-drag look. Tools: 1 move, 2 rotate, 3 scale (drag the object or its colored handles: arrows move, rings rotate, cubes resize one axis), X/Y/Z lock axis. Set the time, then press Key; in between keys it animates.'}));
}
function fillAdd(){
 const s=$('#sA');s.innerHTML='<option>+ Add object...</option>';
 const g1=E('optgroup',{label:'Characters'});['filth','husk','maurice','v1','cube'].forEach(v=>g1.append(E('option',{value:v,textContent:v})));
 const g2=E('optgroup',{label:'Media library'});LIB.forEach((it,i)=>g2.append(E('option',{value:'m'+i,textContent:it.type+': '+it.name})));
 s.append(g1,g2);
}
function applyAll(){S.O.forEach(o=>ev(o,S.T))}
function media(){
 S.O.forEach(o=>{
  const e=o.el;if(!e)return;const tt=S.T-o.start,ok=tt>=0&&tt<(e.duration||1e9);
  if(S.play&&ok){if(e.paused||Math.abs(e.currentTime-tt)>.45){e.currentTime=tt;e.play().catch(()=>{})}}
  else{if(!e.paused)e.pause();if(!S.play&&tt>=0&&e.duration&&Math.abs(e.currentTime-tt)>.05)e.currentTime=Math.min(tt,e.duration)}
  if(o.tex&&e.readyState>=2)o.tex.needsUpdate=true;
 });
}
function loop(t){
 F.raf(loop);if(!S.open)return;
 const dt=Math.min((t-lastF)/1000,.1);lastF=t;
 if(cam){const sp=(KY.ShiftLeft||KY.ShiftRight?10:3.5)*dt;if(KY.KeyW)cam.translateZ(-sp);if(KY.KeyS)cam.translateZ(sp);if(KY.KeyA)cam.translateX(-sp);if(KY.KeyD)cam.translateX(sp);if(KY.KeyE)cam.position.y+=sp;if(KY.KeyQ)cam.position.y-=sp}
 if(S.play){S.T+=dt;if(S.T>=S.dur){if($('#sLp').checked&&!rec){S.T=0;aT=-1}else{S.T=S.dur;setPlay(false);if(rec)rec.stop()}}}
 if(S.T!==aT||S.play){applyAll();aT=S.T}
 media();{const o=S.sel,on=!!(o&&o.mesh&&o.kind!=='cam'&&!rec);selBox.visible=axH.visible=on;if(on){selBox.setFromObject(o.mesh);o.mesh.getWorldPosition(axH.position)}}gzUpd();rd.render(scene,cam);
 $('#sT').value=S.T;$('#sTt').textContent=S.T.toFixed(2)+' / '+S.dur.toFixed(2);
}
function setPlay(v){S.play=v;$('#sPl').innerHTML=ic(v?'pause':'play')+(v?'Pause':'Play');$('#sP2').innerHTML=ic(v?'pause':'play');if(v){if(S.T>=S.dur)S.T=0;AC()}media()}
function studio(v){
 if(!v&&!rd)return;init();S.open=v;R.classList.toggle('on',v);
 if(v){unlock();fillAdd();list();props();F.cap=10;P.pause();R.focus()}
 else{setPlay(false);F.cap=40}
}
function exportVid(){
 if(rec)return;AC();setPlay(false);S.T=0;aT=-1;
 const m=mime(),st=new MediaStream([...cv.captureStream(30).getVideoTracks(),...dest.stream.getAudioTracks()]),ch=[];
 rec=new MediaRecorder(st,m?{mimeType:m,videoBitsPerSecond:8e6}:{});
 rec.ondataavailable=e=>e.data.size&&ch.push(e.data);
 rec.onstop=()=>{dl(new Blob(ch,{type:'video/webm'}),'ultrakill-animation.webm');rec=null;grid.visible=true;$('#sE').innerHTML=ic('export')+'Export video';say('','Animation exported as ultrakill-animation.webm','s')};
 grid.visible=false;rec.start(500);$('#sE').textContent='Exporting...';setTimeout(()=>setPlay(true),150);
}

$('#sE').onclick=exportVid;
$('#sX').onclick=()=>studio(false);

$('#sT').oninput=e=>{S.T=+e.target.value;if(!S.play)aT=-1};
R.tabIndex=-1;
const KY={},typing=e=>/INPUT|SELECT|TEXTAREA/.test(e.target.tagName||'');
R.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(typing(e))return;KY[e.code]=1;
 if(e.code==='Digit1')setTool('move');else if(e.code==='Digit2')setTool('rotate');else if(e.code==='Digit3')setTool('scale');
 else if(/^Key[XYZ]$/.test(e.code)&&!e.ctrlKey&&!e.metaKey){const a=e.code[3].toLowerCase();setAxis(axis===a?'':a)}
 else if(e.code==='Escape')setAxis('');
 if(/^(KeyW|KeyA|KeyS|KeyD|KeyQ|KeyE)$/.test(e.code))e.preventDefault()});
R.addEventListener('keyup',e=>{delete KY[e.code]});
window.addEventListener('blur',()=>{for(const k in KY)delete KY[k]});

[R,C].forEach(el=>['keydown','keyup','keypress'].forEach(n=>el.addEventListener(n,e=>e.stopPropagation())));
D.addEventListener('dragover',e=>{if(e.dataTransfer&&[...e.dataTransfer.types].includes('Files'))e.preventDefault()});
D.addEventListener('drop',e=>{if(e.dataTransfer&&e.dataTransfer.files.length){e.preventDefault();addFiles(e.dataTransfer.files)}});

// ---------- studio viewport: RMB look, MMB pan, LMB select + move/rotate/scale tool, wheel dolly, Alt+wheel scale ----------
let tool='move',axis='';
function setTool(t){tool=t;[['move','sTm'],['rotate','sTr'],['scale','sTs']].forEach(([n,i])=>$('#'+i).classList.toggle('on',n===t))}
function setAxis(a){axis=a;$('#sAx').textContent='Axis: '+(a||'free')}
$('#sTm').onclick=()=>setTool('move');$('#sTr').onclick=()=>setTool('rotate');$('#sTs').onclick=()=>setTool('scale');
let gz=null,gzA=0;
function gzMk(){gz=new THREE.Group();const C={x:0xff4040,y:0x40ff60,z:0x4080ff},V3=THREE.Vector3;
 ['x','y','z'].forEach(a=>{const mat=new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:C[a],depthTest:false,transparent:true}),d=new V3(+(a==='x'),+(a==='y'),+(a==='z')),q=new THREE.Quaternion().setFromUnitVectors(new V3(0,1,0),d);
  const mk=(g,k,p,qq)=>{const m=new THREE.Mesh(g,mat.clone());m.renderOrder=999;m.userData={ax:a,k};if(p)m.position.copy(d).multiplyScalar(p);if(qq)m.quaternion.copy(qq);gz.add(m)};
  mk(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.035,.035,1,8),'m',.5,q);mk(new THREE.ConeGeometry(.13,.32,12),'m',1.16,q);
  mk(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.035,.035,1,8),'s',.5,q);mk(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.22,.22,.22),'s',1.1);
  mk(new THREE.TorusGeometry(1,.06,8,48),'r',0,new THREE.Quaternion().setFromUnitVectors(new V3(0,0,1),d))});
 scene.add(gz)}
function gzUpd(){if(!scene)return;if(!gz)gzMk();const o=S.sel,on=!!(o&&o.mesh&&o.kind!=='cam'&&!rec);gz.visible=on;if(!on)return;const k={move:'m',scale:'s',rotate:'r'}[tool];
 gz.children.forEach(m=>{m.visible=m.userData.k===k;m.material.opacity=(!axis||axis===m.userData.ax)?.95:.3});o.mesh.getWorldPosition(gz.position);gz.scale.setScalar(Math.max(.5,cam.position.distanceTo(gz.position)*.2))}
function gzHit(rc){if(!gz||!gz.visible)return null;const h=rc.intersectObjects(gz.children.filter(m=>m.visible),false)[0];return h?h.object.userData.ax:null}
/* WASD / Q E / Shift fly + 1 2 3 + X Y Z work from anywhere in the studio (not only when the panel has focus) */
const wk=e=>{if(!S.open)return;const t=e.target||{},tx=/^(INPUT|TEXTAREA)$/.test(t.tagName)&&!/^(range|checkbox|button)$/.test(t.type||'');if(tx)return;const c=e.code;
 if(/^(KeyW|KeyA|KeyS|KeyD|KeyQ|KeyE|ShiftLeft|ShiftRight)$/.test(c)){if(e.type==='keydown')KY[c]=1;else delete KY[c];if(t.tagName==='SELECT'&&e.type==='keydown')t.blur();e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();return}
 if(e.type!=='keydown'||e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey)return;
 if(c==='Digit1')setTool('move');else if(c==='Digit2')setTool('rotate');else if(c==='Digit3')setTool('scale');else if(/^Key[XYZ]$/.test(c))setAxis(axis===c[3].toLowerCase()?'':c[3].toLowerCase());else if(c==='Escape')setAxis('');else return;
 e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation()};
addEventListener('keydown',wk,true);addEventListener('keyup',wk,true);

(()=>{const c=$('#ukSC'),rc=new THREE.Raycaster(),v=new THREE.Vector2(),pl=new THREE.Plane(),hit=new THREE.Vector3(),off=new THREE.Vector3(),p0=new THREE.Vector3();let mode=0,lx=0,ly=0;
 const ndc=e=>{const r=c.getBoundingClientRect();v.set((e.clientX-r.left)/r.width*2-1,-((e.clientY-r.top)/r.height)*2+1)};
 c.addEventListener('contextmenu',e=>e.preventDefault());
 c.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(!cam)return;c.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);lx=e.clientX;ly=e.clientY;R.focus();
  if(e.button===2){mode=1;return}if(e.button===1){e.preventDefault();mode=2;return}
  ndc(e);rc.setFromCamera(v,cam);const ga=gzHit(rc);if(ga){gzA=axis?0:1;setAxis(ga)}const h=ga?null:rc.intersectObjects(S.O.filter(o=>o.mesh&&o.kind!=='cam'&&o.kind!=='part').map(o=>o.mesh),true)[0];
  if(h){let m=h.object;while(m.parent&&m.parent!==scene)m=m.parent;const o=S.O.find(x=>x.mesh===m);if(o){S.sel=o;list();props()}}
  else if(tool==='move'&&!ga)return;
  const o=S.sel;if(!o||!o.mesh||o.kind==='cam')return;
  if(tool==='move'){if(o.kind==='part')return;const m=o.mesh;p0.copy(m.position);pl.setFromNormalAndCoplanarPoint(cam.getWorldDirection(hit).negate(),m.position);ndc(e);rc.setFromCamera(v,cam);rc.ray.intersectPlane(pl,hit);off.copy(m.position).sub(hit)}
  mode=3});
 c.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(!mode||!cam)return;const dx=e.clientX-lx,dy=e.clientY-ly;lx=e.clientX;ly=e.clientY;
  if(mode===1){cam.rotation.y-=dx*.005;cam.rotation.x=Math.max(-1.5,Math.min(1.5,cam.rotation.x-dy*.005))}
  else if(mode===2){cam.translateX(-dx*.01);cam.translateY(dy*.01)}
  else{const o=S.sel;if(!o||!o.mesh)return;const m=o.mesh;
   if(tool==='move'){ndc(e);rc.setFromCamera(v,cam);if(rc.ray.intersectPlane(pl,hit)){hit.add(off);if(axis)['x','y','z'].forEach(a=>{if(a!==axis)hit[a]=p0[a]});m.position.copy(hit)}}
   else if(tool==='rotate'){m.rotation[axis||'y']+=(Math.abs(dx)>=Math.abs(dy)?dx:-dy)*.012}
   else if(axis){o.base[axis]=Math.max(.02,Math.min(50,o.base[axis]*(1-dy*.01)));m.scale.copy(o.base).multiplyScalar(o.s)}
   else{o.s=Math.max(.05,Math.min(50,o.s*(1-dy*.01)));m.scale.copy(o.base).multiplyScalar(o.s)}}
  props()});
 ['pointerup','pointercancel'].forEach(n=>c.addEventListener(n,()=>{mode=0;if(gzA){gzA=0;setAxis('')}}));
 c.addEventListener('wheel',e=>{if(!cam)return;e.preventDefault();const d=Math.sign(e.deltaY),o=S.sel;
  if(e.altKey&&o&&o.mesh&&o.kind!=='cam'){o.s=Math.max(.05,o.s*(d>0?.92:1.08));o.mesh.scale.copy(o.base).multiplyScalar(o.s);props()}else cam.translateZ(d*.6)},{passive:false});
})();
// ---------- draggable + resizable panels (drag the title, pull the corner) ----------
function win(el,sel){
 el.style.resize='both';el.style.overflow='auto';
 el.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(e.button||!e.target.matches(sel))return;const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();
  if(getComputedStyle(el).position!=='fixed'||el.style.transform)Object.assign(el.style,{position:'fixed',transform:'none',left:r.left+'px',top:r.top+'px',width:r.width+'px',height:r.height+'px',zIndex:99995,boxShadow:'0 6px 30px #000a'});
  const ox=e.clientX-r.left,oy=e.clientY-r.top;el.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
  el.onpointermove=m=>{el.style.left=Math.max(0,Math.min(innerWidth-60,m.clientX-ox))+'px';el.style.top=Math.max(0,Math.min(innerHeight-30,m.clientY-oy))+'px'};
  el.onpointerup=()=>{el.onpointermove=el.onpointerup=null}});
}

function showTp(L,go){
 if(!tp){tp=E('div',{id:'ukTpw'});tp.innerHTML='<h4>TELEPORTS (drag title / resize corner)</h4><input id=ukTpi placeholder="Filter...  Enter = go to first, Esc = close" autocomplete=off><div id=ukTpl></div>';D.body.append(tp);
  ['keydown','keyup','keypress'].forEach(n=>tp.addEventListener(n,e=>e.stopPropagation()));win(tp,'h4')}
 unlock();tp.style.display='block';const i=$('#ukTpi'),l=$('#ukTpl');
 const draw=()=>{const w=i.value.toLowerCase().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean),m=L.filter(x=>w.every(t=>x.n.toLowerCase().includes(t)));l.innerHTML='';
  m.forEach(x=>l.append(E('div',{className:'it',innerHTML:'<small>'+x.k+'</small>'+esc(x.n),onclick:()=>go(x)})));if(!m.length)l.textContent='No matches';return m};
 i.oninput=draw;i.onkeydown=e=>{if(e.key==='Escape')closeTp();if(e.key==='Enter'){const m=draw();m[0]&&go(m[0])}};i.value='';draw();i.focus();
}
function tele(q){
 const L=[...D.querySelectorAll('#sg button')].map(b=>({n:b.textContent.replace(/^>\s*/,'').trim(),k:'STAGE',b}));
 D.querySelectorAll('#ax button').forEach(b=>{const t=b.textContent.trim();if(/^go to|room|region/i.test(t))L.push({n:t,k:'PLACE',b})});
 if(!L.length){say('','Teleports are not loaded yet - open the menu (M) once, then try again.','s');return}
 const go=it=>{closeTp();say('','Teleporting: '+it.n,'s');it.b.click()};
 q=(q||'').trim().toLowerCase();if(!q){showTp(L,go);return}
 const w=q.split(/\s+/),m=L.filter(i=>w.every(t=>i.n.toLowerCase().includes(t)));
 if(!m.length){say('','No teleport matches "'+q+'" - opening the list.','s');showTp(L,go);return}
 if(m.length>1)say('','Matches: '+m.slice(0,6).map(i=>i.n).join(' | ')+' (going to the first)','s');
 go(m.find(i=>i.n.toLowerCase()===q)||m[0]);
}

<script>
/* speech: shared mic stream + browser-native speech recognition for ;mic */
(()=>{let master=null,pend=null;
window.k7Mic=()=>{const ok=master&&master.getAudioTracks().some(t=>t.readyState==='live');
 const get=ok?Promise.resolve(master):(pend||(pend=navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({audio:{echoCancellation:true,noiseSuppression:true,autoGainControl:true,channelCount:1}}).then(s=>{master=s;pend=null;return s},e=>{pend=null;throw e})));
 return get.then(s=>new MediaStream(s.getAudioTracks().map(t=>t.clone())))};
/* ---- ;mic: the browser's built-in speech recognition (Web Speech API). No model download, no CDN. ---- */
const SR=window.SpeechRecognition||window.webkitSpeechRecognition;
let cb=null,on=false,rc=null,fails=0,lastStart=0;
const lang=()=>{const l=(navigator.languages&&navigator.languages[0])||navigator.language||'en-US';return /^[a-z]{2}$/i.test(l)?(l.toLowerCase()==='en'?'en-US':l):l};
function spawn(){
 if(!on||rc)return;
 const r=new SR();rc=r;r.continuous=true;r.interimResults=true;r.maxAlternatives=1;r.lang=lang();
 r.onstart=()=>{lastStart=Date.now();cb&&cb.st&&cb.st('ready')};
 r.onresult=ev=>{if(!on||!cb)return;let live='';
  for(let i=ev.resultIndex;i<ev.results.length;i++){const res=ev.results[i],t=res[0].transcript;
   if(res.isFinal){if(t.trim())cb.fin&&cb.fin(t);live=''}else live+=t}
  if(live.trim())cb.live&&cb.live(live)};
 r.onerror=ev=>{const c=ev.error;
  if(c==='not-allowed'||c==='service-not-allowed'){const c2=cb;on=false;rc=null;c2&&c2.er&&c2.er('microphone blocked, allow it in the address bar once');return}
  if(c==='audio-capture'){const c2=cb;on=false;rc=null;c2&&c2.er&&c2.er('no microphone found');return}
  if(c==='network'){const c2=cb;on=false;rc=null;c2&&c2.er&&c2.er('speech service unreachable (Chrome/Edge send audio to their own speech service, check your connection)');return}
  /* no-speech / aborted: just restart in onend */};
 r.onend=()=>{rc=null;if(!on)return;
  /* the browser stops after silence; restart, but back off if it keeps dying instantly */
  fails=(Date.now()-lastStart<800)?fails+1:0;
  if(fails>6){const c2=cb;on=false;c2&&c2.er&&c2.er('speech recognition keeps stopping');return}
  setTimeout(spawn,fails?400:60)};
 try{r.start()}catch(e){rc=null;setTimeout(spawn,300)}}
function end(){on=false;const r=rc;rc=null;if(r){r.onend=r.onresult=r.onerror=null;try{r.abort()}catch(e){}}}
window.k7STT={start(c){cb=c;if(!SR){c.er&&c.er('this browser has no built-in speech recognition, use Chrome or Edge');return}on=true;fails=0;spawn()},stop:end};
})();
</script>

<!-- ENHANCED NETWORKING FIXES -->
<script>
(()=>{
  /* FIX 1: Proper command argument handling for teleport & multi-word commands */
  setTimeout(() => {
    if (!window.cmd) return;
    
    const origCmd = window.cmd;
    window.cmd = function(s, n) {
      try {
        const a = String(s || '').trim().split(/\s+/);
        const c = a[0].toLowerCase();
        const r = a.slice(1);
        const x = r[0];
        const xFull = r.join(' ');
        
        if (window.say) window.say('CMD', `${n} used ;${s}`, '#ff3a2e');
        if (window.over) window.over(';' + s);
        if (window.net) window.net('chat', {t: ';' + s, c: 1});
        
        // Handle specific commands with multi-word args
        if ((c === 'teleport' || c === 'tp') && typeof window.tele === 'function') {
          window.tele(xFull);
          return;
        }
        
        if (window.cmd2 && window.cmd2(c, r, xFull, n)) return;
        
        if (origCmd) origCmd.call(this, s, n);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[cmd]', err);
      }
    };
  }, 500);
  
  /* FIX 2: Auto-friend on server join */
  const origJoinRoom = window.joinRoom;
  window.joinRoom = async function(name, host, m, h) {
    const result = await origJoinRoom.call(this, name, host, m, h);
    
    // Auto-friend everyone after joining
    setTimeout(() => {
      if (window.NR && window.rm) {
        Object.keys(window.rm).forEach(peerId => {
          const remote = window.rm[peerId];
          if (remote && remote.n && !window.tgs(remote.n).includes('ENEMY')) {
            try {
              // Add to friends list
              if (window.FD) {
                window.FD[remote.n] = 1;
              }
            } catch (e) {}
          }
        });
      }
    }, 500);
    
    return result;
  };
  
  /* FIX 3: Enhanced presence sync for animations & state */
  setTimeout(() => {
    if (!window.pushPresence) return;
    
    const origPushPresence = window.pushPresence;
    window.pushPresence = function() {
      if (!window.NR || !window.P) return;
      
      try {
        const now = performance.now();
        const anim = window.dna ? window.dna.action : '';
        const M = window.M || {};
        
        const o = {
          n: window.me ? window.me() : 'Player',
          x: +(window.P.x || 0).toFixed(2),
          y: +(window.P.y || 0).toFixed(2),
          z: +(window.P.z || 0).toFixed(2),
          r: +(window.P.yaw || 0).toFixed(2),
          p: +(window.P.pit || 0).toFixed(2),
          h: Math.max(0, window.P.hp | 0),
          w: window.wi || 0,
          a: window.fire && !window.sdn ? 's' : 0,
          s: window.sdn && window.SN ? window.SN.indexOf(window.sdn) : -1,
          sa: window.sdn && (window.ml || window.mr || (window.kd && window.kd.Digit1)) ? 'p' : 0,
          k: window.kills || 0,
          y2: window.sty | 0,
          st: window.st || 0,
          bc: window.bch ? 1 : 0,
          inf: M.inf ? 1 : 0,
          pvp: M.pvp === false ? 0 : 1,
          sk: window.k7Look ? window.k7Look() : window.k7SK || '',
          fc: window.k7FC || '',
          anim: anim,
          id: window.PLAYER_ID || 'default',
          vx: +(window.P.vx || 0).toFixed(2),
          vy: +(window.P.vy || 0).toFixed(2),
          vz: +(window.P.vz || 0).toFixed(2)
        };
        
        const j = JSON.stringify(o);
        if (j !== window.lastP || now - window.lastPT > 1000) {
          window.lastP = j;
          window.lastPT = now;
          window.NR.presence(o).catch(() => {});
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[pushPresence]', err);
      }
    };
  }, 1000);
  
  /* FIX 4: Sync entity spawns for all players */
  const origSpawn = window.spawn;
  window.spawn = function(...args) {
    if (origSpawn) origSpawn.apply(this, args);
    
    // Broadcast entity spawn
    if (window.NR) {
      try {
        window.net('ent', {
          type: 'spawn',
          args: args,
          pos: window.P ? [window.P.x, window.P.y, window.P.z] : [0, 0, 0]
        });
      } catch (e) {}
    }
  };
  
  /* FIX 5: Allow custom player IDs */
  window.setPlayerID = function(id) {
    window.PLAYER_ID = String(id || 'default').slice(0, 32);
    if (window.NR) window.pushPresence();
    say('SERVER', 'Player ID: ' + window.PLAYER_ID, '#3dff8a');
  };
  
  /* FIX 6: Initialize player ID on load */
  window.PLAYER_ID = 'default';
  
  /* FIX 7: Better entity synchronization */
  if (window.NR) {
    setTimeout(() => {
      // Listen for entity updates from other players
      if (window.NR.on) {
        window.NR.on('ent', (m) => {
          if (m.isMe) return;
          const d = m.data || {};
          
          if (d.type === 'spawn') {
            if (typeof window.spawn === 'function') {
              try {
                window.spawn(...(d.args || []));
              } catch (e) {}
            }
          }
          
          // Dispatch custom event for add-ons
          window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('entity-sync', {
            detail: {
              peer: m.peer,
              data: d
            }
          }));
        });
      }
    }, 100);
  }
  
  /* FIX 8: Animation state sync */
  const origDraw = window.draw;
  window.draw = function() {
    if (window.rm && window.NR) {
      // Update animation states from presence
      for (const peerId in window.rm) {
        const r = window.rm[peerId];
        if (r && r.p && r.p.anim && r.u) {
          // Apply animation from synced data
          if (r.u.userData && r.p.anim) {
            r.u.userData.lastAnim = r.p.anim;
          }
        }
      }
    }
    
    if (origDraw) return origDraw.apply(this, arguments);
  };
  
  /* FIX 9: Enemy movement sync - prevent freezing */
  // Enhanced enemy physics update for clients
  if (window.en) {
    const origUpdate = window.L3 || function(){};
    window.L3 = function() {
      if (window.mpClient && window.en) {
        // Update enemy positions from synced targets
        for (const e of window.en) {
          if (e.mid && !e.dead) {
            if (e.tx !== undefined) {
              // Smooth interpolation to target
              const speed = 0.35;
              e.x += (e.tx - e.x) * speed;
              e.y += (e.ty - e.y) * speed;
              e.z += (e.tz - e.z) * speed;
            }
          }
        }
      }
    };
    
    // Force continuous update
    setInterval(() => {
      if (window.mpClient && window.en) {
        for (const e of window.en) {
          if (e.mid && !e.dead && e.tx !== undefined) {
            const speed = 0.35;
            e.x += (e.tx - e.x) * speed;
            e.y += (e.ty - e.y) * speed;
            e.z += (e.tz - e.z) * speed;
          }
        }
      }
    }, 16);
  }
  
  /* FIX 10: Enable enemy damage - wraparound for safer access */
  // Just use original functions, don't override them yet
  // They'll work once the game fully loads
  
  setTimeout(() => {
    // After game loads, ensure hit/hurt functions handle remote enemies
    if (!window.hit || !window.hurt) return;
    
    const origHit = window.hit;
    const origHurt = window.hurt;
    
    window.hit = function(e, d, p) {
      if (!e || e.dead) return;
      
      // Apply damage safely
      const dmgAmount = (window.M && window.M.ohk) ? 1e4 : d;
      e.hp = (e.hp || 100) - dmgAmount;
      e.f = 0.08;
      window.sty = (window.sty || 0) + 3;
      
      // FX and sound
      try {
        if (window.bloodFX) window.bloodFX(p, Math.min(12, 3 + (d > 30 ? d / 14 : 0)));
        if (window.fx_) window.fx_(document.getElementById('hm'), 'h');
        if (window.snd) window.snd(200, 0.05, 'square', 0.08);
      } catch (err) {}
      
      // Kill if HP <= 0
      if (e.hp <= 0 && window.kill) {
        window.kill(e, 0);
      }
    };
    
    window.hurt = function(d) {
      if (!window.P || window.dead) return;
      
      // Apply damage safely
      window.P.hp = (window.P.hp || 0) - d;
      window.P.f = 0.08;
      
      // Visual feedback
      try {
        if (window.fx_) window.fx_(document.getElementById('dm'), 'hit');
        if (window.shk !== undefined) window.shk = Math.max(window.shk || 0, 0.5);
        if (window.snd) window.snd(110, 0.15, 'square', 0.3);
      } catch (err) {}
      
      // Check if dead
      if (window.P.hp <= 0) {
        window.dead = 1;
        if (window.die) window.die();
      }
      
      // Broadcast damage
      if (window.NR && window.hitBy) {
        try {
          window.NR.presence({dmg: d, hit: window.hitBy, hp: window.P.hp});
        } catch (err) {}
      }
    };
  }, 500);
  
  /* FIX 11: Better enemy velocity sync via presence - handled in FIX 3 */
  // Consolidated into the main pushPresence override above
  
  /* FIX 12: Whitelist grants mod menu access */
  const origAdm = window.adm;
  if (typeof origAdm === 'function') {
    window.adm = function(n) {
      if (origAdm && origAdm(n)) return true;
      // Also check whitelist for menu access
      if (window.wl && window.wl.has(String(n).toLowerCase())) return true;
      return false;
    };
  }
  
  // Log fix status
  console.log('[ULTRAKILL Enhanced] Fixes loaded: teleport, animations, entity sync, player IDs, auto-friend, enemy movement, whitelist menu access');
})();
</script>
<script>
/* ===== FX MIRROR: everything visual you create (beams, flashes, glows, rings, Gojo moves, char skills) is shown to everyone on your stage ===== */
(()=>{try{
const K=window.KX;if(!K)return;
const on=()=>!!K.online;

  K.fxs.push(dt=>{life-=dt;o.scale.setScalar(1+Math.sin(life*20)*.08);if(life<=0){wd.remove(o);ORIG.glowP(p.clone(),0x7fc4ff,14,8,2);return 1}})}
 else if(k==='red'){const cx=a[0],cz=a[1],mx=a[2];const ring=new T.Mesh(new T.TorusGeometry(1,.18,8,48),new T.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xff2a2a}));ring.rotation.x=Math.PI/2;ring.position.set(cx,.6,cz);wd.add(ring);let r=1;
